# Coin Pilot Scorecard

| Area | Definition of done | Current evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| Strategy contract | Completed-candle oversold reaction, delayed revalidation, bounded exits | Source + unit tests | PASS |
| Web UI | Desktop dashboard, responsive mobile layout, paper status controls | Browser/API smoke, strict/relaxed/MFE-MAE evidence display, and PWA assets | PASS |
| Installable app | Manifest, icons, service worker, standalone metadata | Static manifest/service-worker checks | PASS |
| Historical validation | Fee/slippage-aware walk-forward holdout with minimum trades/PF/return/MDD | Fresh 7-day fixed-config top-20 run: 0/20 promoted; 17 training-gate failures, 3 holdout-gate failures | HOLD |
| Forward paper | Isolated multi-market ledger with telemetry, snapshots, durable strict trades, and continuity gate | v71 baseline and v72 0.25% candidate are preserved; v73 is the active latest-code 0.15% baseline with MFE/MAE telemetry, currently 10 cycles with no trades | IN PROGRESS |
| Live safety | Live scalping blocked until global validation promotion | `assertLiveValidationGate()` requires matching `fixed_config` report; current tuned/failed report blocked | PASS |
| Profitability | Forward thresholds met and independently reviewed | No qualifying evidence yet | NOT PROVEN |
| Continuity | Heartbeat gaps over the configured limit invalidate promotion | v71 owner loss was fail-closed as orphan and resumed with the 482s gap preserved; risk/analysis continuity is currently healthy after recovery | IN PROGRESS |

## Latest evidence

- `2026-09-14` review-fix pass: the rejected change set is now corrected — shared `historicalTimestampForCandle` parses epoch-ms numbers/strings across backtest + research lanes, `monitorOpenPositions()` passes the winnerShadow exit override on the 1s risk path (regression test proves a same-aged `shadow` book still closes `MAX_HOLD_TIME` while `winnerShadow` extends), and the no-trade-fill CLI reached env parity with sibling lanes while staying `promoted=false`. Suite `226/226` green; no live-order or promotion boundary changed.
- `2026-09-14` momentum-shadow A/B incident: both owners died during machine sleep (~`03:11Z` stale heartbeat); `momentumShadowStatus.js` now reports `STOPPED:owner_process_missing`/`heartbeat_stale` like the forward-paper status. A concurrent Codex app-server session relaunched both books at `06:23Z`; brief competing relaunches left honest transient 4-market drift records, and the lane is back on the 12-market contract under pids 47508/47509. Regime book recorded its first exits — ETH `-2.0626%`, NEAR `-3.3599%` (`REGIME_OFF`, `-1,145,630 KRW`) — first outcome evidence is negative for regime mode; both books remain diagnostic-only.

- `maxLosingHoldMinutes` fixed-cache holdout sweep (14d BTC/ETH/XRP, 20,160 candles, `/tmp/mlh-sweep-*.json`): baseline `-0.1147%`/23 trades vs 3m `-0.1155%`, 5m `-0.1101%`, 10m `-0.1058%`/22 trades each. - A local no-response HTTP probe now fails in `117ms` with `ECONNABORTED` when configured for a `100ms` timeout. This closes the previously observed unbounded Upbit-socket wait class.
- Historical tuning now requires the selected training candidate to have at least 3 trades, non-negative return, and PF >= 1 before holdout promotion is even considered. Fresh 2-day BTC selected no-trade/zero-return behavior; fresh 7-day BTC had +0.0049% training return but only 1 trade; both were correctly marked `training_gate_failed`.

A 1-day RAY exploratory run produced no viable training candidate (`training_gate_failed`), so it remains disabled and needs a longer holdout before any decision.

- The fixed-config validator now reports `validationMode=fixed_config`, and the UI labels fixed versus tuned reports so a tuned candidate cannot be mistaken for the live runtime settings.
- v8 adds an independent ticker-based position-risk monitor at `1000ms`; a deterministic test proves it can close an over-stop dry-run position without waiting for the full market-analysis cycle.
- v9 extends the same 1-second ticker monitor to shadow books, so diagnostic stop/take/max-hold results are not delayed by the 20-market scan. The shadow close retains the entry rejection reasons for filter attribution. Latest v9 has three open shadow positions and no close yet.
- Top-20 fixed-config report now includes historical rejection telemetry; the dominant training/holdout blockers are previous RSI not oversold, previous high-break failure, and rebound threshold failure.
- Live promotion now requires a `fixed_config` report whose comparable strategy/risk parameters match the current runtime; a promoted tuned report or config-drifted report is rejected.
- The package now exposes `npm run validate:scalping:fixed` as the explicit command for generating the fixed runtime report used by the live gate; `npm run validate:scalping` remains exploratory tuned validation.
- Same-window 1-minute A/B study (20 markets, generated `2026-09-09T19:46:46Z`) removed timestamp drift between candidates: baseline `74` holdout trades / `-0.1806%`, volume `1.5x` `55` / `-0.1322%`, rebound `0.25%` `46` / `-0.0791%`, break-even/trailing `74` / `-0.1239%`, and volume `1.5x` plus protection `55` / `-0.0888%`. Every variant remained `0/20` promoted; runtime defaults were not changed.
- Repeated same-window 1-minute study after the cost-adjusted protection fix (20 markets, generated `2026-09-09T20:36:17Z`) still failed promotion: baseline `75` / `-0.1875%`, volume `1.5x` `57` / `-0.1548%`, rebound `0.25%` `48` / `-0.0937%`, cost-adjusted protection `77` / `-0.1430%`, and volume `1.5x` plus protection `58` / `-0.0996%`. These values are diagnostic and no candidate was enabled.
- Same-window 1-minute rebound-width study found `minReboundPercent=0.5` as a low-sample aggregate positive: `14` holdout trades / `+0.0105%`, while volume `1.5x + rebound 0.5` had `12` / `+0.0065%`. Rebound-0.5 still had `19/20` training-gate failures and remains a forward candidate only; it does not authorize live use.
- Longer same-window 1-minute/30-day six-market study rejected the short-window optimism: baseline `129` / `-0.7842%`, rebound `0.5%` `21` / `-0.0571%`, and volume `1.5x + rebound 0.5%` `15` / `-0.0462%`. The combined candidate is the closest to flat but still negative and under-sampled; v19 is observational only.
- Same-window 1-minute momentum experiment (`momentum_breakout`) produced `530` holdout trades across 20 markets but `-3.7440%` summed return and `0/20` positive markets. It is explicitly diagnostic-only and must not be enabled as a fallback for filter starvation.
- Same-window 30-day/1-minute profile study across six markets found `bb_reclaim -0.5133%`/67 trades and `trend_rebound -0.0797%`/21 trades; neither passed promotion. The existing profile variants remain diagnostic.
- Same-window 5-minute baseline over 7 days was positive only in aggregate (`20` trades, `+0.0341%`) with `18/19` training-gate failures. A longer 30-day/5-minute six-market study was negative (`36` trades, `-0.0529%` baseline; `+0.0108%` rebound-25) and did not pass promotion. The timeframe switch is not justified yet.
- Same-window 15-minute/30-day study across 19 data-sufficient markets remained inconclusive/negative: baseline `9` trades / `-0.0153%`, rebound `0.25%` `6` / `+0.0135%`, protection `9` / `-0.0153%`; all variants were held. A timeframe change is not justified.
- Same-window 30-day exit study rejected lower targets: `SL0.8/TP1.0` `133` / `-0.8858%`, `SL0.8/TP1.2` `132` / `-0.8675%`, `SL1.2/TP1.2` `129` / `-0.8584%`; cost-adjusted protection `131` / `-0.7041%`. No exit candidate was promoted.
- Same-window 30-day hold-time study found `maxHold=5m` `137` / `-0.7542%`, `15m` `133` / `-0.6750%`, and `30m` `130` / `-0.8219%`; shorter hold reduced loss but remained negative and was not promoted.
- Per-cycle batch ticker prefetch is covered by a no-duplicate-data-access test; it reduces the 20-market analysis path's ticker requests from one per market to one batch request while retaining per-market fallback on batch failure. It changes request efficiency only, not the signal contract.
- Same-window 30-day range-floor study found `minSignalRange=0.2%` improved baseline `-0.8065%` to `-0.5743%`, and `0.4%` to `-0.1804%`, but still no promotion. Combining it with volume-1.5/rebound-0.5 changed `-0.0462%` (15 trades) to `-0.0450%` (13 trades) at floor 0.4%; this is a marginal, under-sampled diagnostic result only.
- Same-window 60-day/1-minute six-market study kept the range-floor combination near flat but not profitable: volume-1.5/rebound-0.5 `25` trades / `-0.0004%`, plus floor `0.2%` `24` / `-0.0229%`, plus floor `0.4%` `20` / `+0.0124%`. All six markets failed training gate, so the aggregate positive is not promotable.
- The reusable `npm run compare:scalping` study now fetches each market's candle window once and evaluates multiple fixed candidates on that identical data; its output is diagnostic and separated from `scalping_validation.json`.
- Upbit market and risk clients now share a process-wide request slot; a deterministic test verifies concurrent clients start requests at least 80ms apart, reducing self-inflicted rate-limit collisions without changing trading signals.
- Break-even protection now uses a shared cost-adjusted price helper (`protectionPrices.js`) and includes two fees plus adverse exit slippage; this prevents a label of "break-even" from hiding a predictable net loss. The protection candidate remains disabled in the default runtime until a holdout and forward gate pass.
- Environment parsing now preserves intentional zero values for valid scalping experiments (volume/close/trend/range/protection offsets), and `npm run paper:forward` now carries the documented paper minimum-days/trades/return/MDD thresholds into the ledger instead of silently falling back to code defaults.
- A 7-day SC range-guard study (`maxSignalRangePercent=1.0`) also failed training/holdout gates: training 2 trades/+0.0423%, holdout 2 trades/-0.0078%/PF 0. It remains diagnostic-only.
- A 2-day BTC exploratory run with explicit `SCALP_RSI_OVERSOLD=30` produced 0 training/holdout trades and `training_gate_failed`; the dedicated override is wired and snapshot-visible, but no RSI threshold is promoted from this sample.
